# Graph Report - doctor-ports  (2026-07-30)

## Corpus Check
- 1635 files · ~2,073,631 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 29643 nodes · 56302 edges · 1365 communities (1228 shown, 137 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6914 edges (avg confidence: 0.74)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `69dea391`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
